PASS __tests__/unit/pdpa-erasure-service.test.js
  pdpa-erasure-service
    requestErasure
      √ rejects when healthId is missing (2 ms)
      √ rejects when actorId is missing (1 ms)
      √ rejects unknown data subject (2 ms)
      √ rejects when legalHold is set (1 ms)
      √ returns requestId + expiresAt + token; persists envelope; emits audit + fanout (3 ms)
      √ returns PDPA_ERASURE_IN_PROGRESS when a REQUESTED envelope already exists (1 ms)
    confirmErasure
      √ rejects when token mismatches (2 ms)
      √ rejects and marks EXPIRED when the window has passed (8 ms)
      √ rejects when requestId does not match the envelope (1 ms)
      √ executes erasure on valid token and returns the summary (3 ms)
    executeErasure
      √ anonymises user, application formData, and certificates; deletes drafts + notifications (4 ms)
      √ blocks erasure when legalHold is set on the user row (1 ms)
      √ requires either healthId or userId (1 ms)
    _sanitizeApplicationFormData
      √ clears known PII keys at every level of a nested blob (2 ms)
      √ passes through null / primitive inputs unchanged (1 ms)
    listErasureRequests
      √ returns envelopes without the token field (2 ms)
      √ filters by status when provided (1 ms)
      √ falls back to a broad scan when JSON-path filter is unsupported (1 ms)
    cancelErasure
      √ flips status to CANCELED and strips the token (2 ms)
      √ refuses to cancel a non-REQUESTED envelope (1 ms)
    STAGE 0 — erasure gap + data-state-agnostic resolve
      √ (d) executeErasure ALSO nulls the *Hmac columns + sets canonicalId = user.id (3 ms)
      √ (d) anonymises the personal INDIVIDUAL Entity row (null thaiCitizenId + thaiCitizenIdHash) (3 ms)
      √ (d) Application anonymise queries by the post-cascade FK value (resolvedUserId), not the old national ID (3 ms)
      √ (b) requestErasure resolves the subject by the national-ID HASH (not a canonicalId value WHERE) (1 ms)
    M1 — erasure and the holder columns (D8 / AC4)
      √ asks Postgres for holderType — without it every branch below reads undefined (2 ms)
      √ wipes holderDisplayName to the sentinel when holderType is LEGACY_PERSON (a person is behind that name) (2 ms)
      √ wipes holderDisplayName to the sentinel when holderType is INDIVIDUAL (a person is behind that name) (2 ms)
      √ never writes holderDisplayName at all when holderType is JURISTIC (AC4 — the farm keeps its certificate) (2 ms)
      √ never writes holderDisplayName at all when holderType is COMMUNITY_ENTERPRISE (AC4 — the farm keeps its certificate) (2 ms)
      √ leaves holderDisplayName alone for a row whose holderType is still NULL (pre-backfill safety) (2 ms)
